| 8 | Vitamin D / supplements | PASS-LIVE | **Ron-originated decision:** use D3+K2 together. **Assistant-updated concrete product after bioavailability + ROI review:** **Nutraxin Vitamin D3K2 Sprey 30 ml**. Official composition per puff: D3 (cholecalciferol) **1000 IU / 25 µg** + K2 **MK-7 25 µg**; official international Nutraxin ingredient page lists **extra virgin olive oil** as the carrier. Current Nar Ecza listing indicates ~207 puffs and ~233 TL, with product search also surfacing ~224 TL offers. Direct clinical head-to-head data for this exact Nutraxin spray vs Nutraxin tablet were not found, so no numeric absorption superiority is claimed. General RCT evidence shows ordinary oral spray and capsule vitamin D3 are often equally effective, oil drops and tablets can be bioequivalent, while special nanoemulsion formulations can outperform conventional capsules; therefore dosage/formulation/meal context matter more than the word 'spray'. The spray is adopted because it is at least absorption-robust, uses an oil vehicle appropriate for fat-soluble vitamins, provides ~207 vs 120 daily doses at a similar total purchase price, and has the same D3+MK-7 dose as the tablet. Execution: 1 puff daily with/after the 12:00 meal once physically purchased. TickTick procurement and daily reminder are synchronized. |

Chicken safety endpoint remains **74°C / 165°F measured with a food thermometer**; current official USDA/FSIS guidance says a thermometer is the only sure way to verify that poultry reached a bacteria-destroying temperature, while appearance/doneness cues are not reliable safety tests. Ron directly reported on 2026-09-23 that he purchased a food thermometer, but later corrected that it is a different model than the earlier pictured Bestac TP-300; exact model is currently UNKNOWN. The 74°C chicken endpoint is instrumented for execution as long as the device can measure internal food temperature with a probe. First-use sanity checking in ice water remains advisable; actual instrument accuracy is still empirical. Prompt shallow cooling and <=4°C fridge target remain current; leftovers should be refrigerated promptly, divided into shallow containers, and generally used refrigerated within 3–4 days or frozen. Mon–Wed chicken stays refrigerated from the Sunday batch; Thu–Sat chicken is cooked Wednesday. Lentils: ~400 g Sun–Wed refrigerated and ~300 g Thu–Sat frozen after cooling, thawed in fridge Wednesday. Hard-cooked eggs remain within the 7-day refrigerated window. |

### BUY-03 olive oil: grams versus liter-priced packs

[FAO/WHO Codex olive-oil standard](https://www.fao.org/4/Y2774E/y2774e04.htm) gives relative density at 20°C ~0.910–0.916 compared with water at 20°C (~0.9982 g/mL), i.e. **~0.90836–0.91435 g/mL** class envelope. For the **DATED 2026-10-24** Komili 1-L listing **529.95 TL** used elsewhere in the model, mass-cost `price × grams / (1000 × density)` yields 5 g **2.898–2.917 TL**, 15 g **8.694–8.751 TL**, 20 g **11.592–11.668 TL**, 35 g **20.286–20.419 TL**, 105 g **60.857–61.258 TL**. These are transparent **conditional** costs, not today's prices. Exact density within this class changes consumed cost by <0.7%, so it is **not** a decision-critical unresolved item at fixed sticker price; 1mL=1g would understate cost. Actual checkout, route/delivery, edible yields, pack losses, other ingredients and time remain UNKNOWN.

**Propagation and boundaries:** No change to any original A/B grams, calories, macros, Na/SFA, omega-3, 3-day scenarios or repeat reminders follows merely from comparing candidates. If an alternate SKU is *actually chosen*, first redo its applicable full-slot/day, micro, salt, cold-chain, packing, waste and cost dependencies. Lor opening life and Duru drained-pack nutrient-table basis remain blockers. BUY-02/03, NUT-01/02, SYS-01 stay OPEN. No transactions, shopping cart, Cronometer, Calendar, reminders or diet A changes were made.

Для яиц категория показывала47.95/6, а точная карточка45.95/6; используем датированную цену карточки, перед покупкой сверить применимый адрес/итог. Указанные на странице70ккал/6Б под общим заголовком100г не подходят для надёжной замены профиля100г cookedegg: основа неоднозначна, donor464674 остаётся моделью. Инструкция страницы относится к сырым яйцам; сваренные требуют прежней холодовой цепи B1, а не хранения при комнатной температуре.

Курица — сырая бескостная грудка, не готовая еда и не разрешение готовить новый дополнительный батч. Общие страницы bonfile30098555 и30097256 закрыты для продажи; найденная карточка Banvit — отдельное предложение, не доказательство наличия закрытых позиций. Не сравнивать его249.90/кг с ценой бёдер/крыльев без костей/кожи/выхода. UI показывает1.00кг по умолчанию, **минимальный оплачиваемый шаг и фактическая упаковка не установлены**.

### B1: завтрак, обед и три дня
- Если m_e — фактическая средняя масса готового очищенного яйца в граммах, то цена **100г = 765.8333/m_e ₺**. Два яйца стоят15.3167 независимо от их размера, но равенство двум яйцам100г остаётся условным. B1-завтрак: **39.52727 +765.8333/m_e +16.5528/y_b ₺** по прежним прочим предложениям. При ровно50г/яйцо это54.84394+16.5528/y_b; это иллюстрация, не измерение.
- Обед B1: **15.62727 +1.1q_chickpea100 +(37.485 либо44.85)/y_h +17.241/y_t +11.494/y_p +430.5/m_p +2.64975/ρ ₺**. Остальные символы определены в предыдущей смете; price-операнды не заменяют неизвестный edible/cooked yield.
- Три B1-дня требуют450г готовой курицы: сырая масса450/y_h г, consumption-cost112.455/y_h с Kart либо134.55/y_h без. При условном y_h=.75 это600г сырой и149.94/179.40 ₺, **не подтверждённый чек/выход**. Готовые безопасные остатки A дают нулевой немедленный cash, а не нулевую распределённую стоимость.
- При двух яйцах ежедневно три дня используют6яиц: одна6-пачка45.95 cash/consumed, без обязательной30-пачки. Это6 из существующих8недельныхяиц A, не6 сверх8. При модели ровно300г готовых яиц за3дня количество и цена зависят от m_e; нет фиктивного точного edibleyield.
- По cooked-donor463083 25г белка из курицы стоили бы **20.21191/y_h ₺ с Kart или24.18311/y_h без**, но белок конкретного приготовленного Banvit не измерен. Не применять сырой показатель белка к готовым150г. Яичный25г-белка cost аналогично зависит от m_e и donor12.58P/100г, не выводится из неоднозначных6Б на сайте.

### Рыба: цена, доставка, порция и недельная идентичность
[Текущая страница производителя](https://www.dardanel.com.tr/2-lezzetlerimiz/191-kulup-kutu-deniz-urunleri/232-canakkale-sardalya/) повторно подтверждает213ккал21.76Б13.76Ж3.9SFA/.4У/1.7соли и1420мгEPA+DHA на100г, описание kılçıksız. Модель остаётся125г целой банки с маслом: **266.25ккал27.2Б17.2Ж4.875SFA/.5У/850мгNa/1775мгEPA+DHA**. Кальций костей не начисляется. Поисковый фрагмент старого marketplace Q&A о kılçıklı не подтверждён прямым чтением; он не перевешивает производителя и не доказывает состав фактической банки. Проверка упаковки/идентичности остаётся эмпирической, не меняет модель автоматически.
- Pazarama-страница магазина показывает155/1 и620/4; однако точное предложение/его доставку и соответствующую упаковку не удалось подтвердить как покупочный итог. **Не подставлять155 ₺ вместо249 в достижимую корзину.** Поисковый155 на Trendyol также расходится с прямым249. Başdaş потребовал адрес и не показал цену; не основание для локального предложения İstanbul.
- 25г белка при целой125г банке27.2Б: **228.86 ₺ при249 + доля доставки**,279.41 при304;251.47 при условной273.60. Это сравнение цены белка, не предложение заменить рыбу орехами/молоком и потерять EPA+DHA.
- Для одной банки база304 с объявленной бесплатной доставкой выигрывает у249+S_f, только если S_f>55 ₺. Условная273.60 — порог S_f>24.60, если скидка применима. Не создавать корзину ради проверки/не покупать ещё продукт для350.
- Минимальный резерв прежний:1банка из недельного A +Etimek148 +2milk200. При покупке новой рыбы справочный subtotal **371.50+S_f ₺** (249+122.50), либо426.50 при304 и объявленной бесплатной доставке **только рыбы**;396.10 лишь при условной273.60. Доставка остальных двух продавцов/адрес неизвестны: ни одно число не полный адресный минимум. Если банка уже есть в A, немедленный cash для её переноса0, её потребление/восстановление не бесплатно.
- Полная ранее рассчитанная B2-fish-cost дельта теперь **235.40762 +16.5528/y_b +S_f ₺** к не-рыбному дню при249; с304 вместо235.40762 используется290.40762, при условной273.60 —260.00762. Три дня1fish+2Y150 =3×не-рыбнаяформула+этадельта, доставка каждой отдельной закупки считается по факту один раз, не автоматически на каждую порцию.
- Четыре банки ради меньшей unitprice не добавлять к резерву: это рост cash/остатков. Две рыбы в неделю сохраняются; дополнительную третью не назначать ради белка/доставки. Открытая банка не shelfstable; B2 съедается целиком в момент открытия, нет полбанки на столе. Производитель допускает до2дней в4°C под маслом после открытия, что не разрешает хранение без холодильника.

### Регрессия и остаточные ограничения
Здесь меняются **ценовые операнды**, а не граммы/рецепты. Для тех же подтверждаемых продуктовых спецификаций дневные и3-дневные энергия/белок/Na/SFA/EPA+DHA дельты0; неоднозначные яичная этикетка, cooked-yield и фактический SKU не объявлены доказанными. Прежние Pro26/52, almond/walnut и yogurt-альтернативы остаются отдельными условными сценариями.
Независимый Python Decimal проверил единицы, порции/3дня, цены25гБ, пороги доставки и резерв; JS повторяет ключевые коэффициенты. Закрыта задача найти публичные альтернативы прежним недоступным яйцам/курице и обновить рыбную справочную смету, **не** полная адресная закупка. Остаются: адресные остатки/checkout-доставка, actualpack/edible/cooked yields, q_chickpea100 для фактических B1остатков, измельчение льна, незаявленные SKUнутриенты/Pro+identity и практическая проверка. Не повторять прежние недоступные293.20курицу/1150яйца как «нет альтернатив». Покупок/платежей/сообщений/изменений A и дневника нет.

## 2026-10-23 — B execution cards and end-to-end safety disposition

**Engineering specification for the already modelled slots; nutritional finalisation still OPEN.** Use B only for the missed A slot, not automatically all five cards. No new food quantities, mandatorydiet, purchase, reminder or consumption record. The earlier lor-heavy queue draft is SUPERSEDED by these existing no-lor models; keep it only as dated history.

### Select before assembling
1. Safe cooked A leftovers and reliable refrigeration available → matching B1 card. Missing safe batch/date or failedcoldchain → do not improvise B1 from doubtful food.
2. No cookedleftovers or no cooling → matching B2 card, with **factory-sealed ready-to-eat shelfstable** products and wholeproduce until eating. A sealed refrigerated product is not shelfstable. Read actual pack instructions.
3. Missing an essential item/Pro+identity unverified → that complete card is unavailable. Use another already available A/B slot only as a labelled partial substitute, without claiming equal calories/protein. Do not compensate blindly with extra lor/nuts/fish/Pro+, and do not open a larger pack without a safe remainder plan. Bought-meal fallback already exists in A; availability and price remain a real-world choice, no order is authorized here.

## 2026-10-23 — B micronutrient transfer: completed bounded analysis, not label closure

No actual intake, supplement use or inventory inferred.

### Reproducible transfer contract
Live Cronometer batch read23Oct returned17existing donor profiles (the16ingredient IDs already listed below plus milk3147). Start from the PR173 known-layer table below, **not an invented zero-filled total**. Two missing contributions are screened:

B1 already contains its chickpea110g and other donors; add only the milk sensitivity. FishB2 receives milk only, **no chickpea**, no fictitious fish-bone calcium. Pro+ undisclosed minerals/choline are not imputed from ordinary milk or protein concentration. ### Consequence for finalisation and safety
[NIH choline](https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/Choline-Consumer/) lists adultmaleAI550mg/day. The~195mg/day partial sensitivity cannot establish adequacy; the omitted Pro+/bread/fish fields might materially increase it, so this is **not a diagnosed deficiency**. B1 retains eggs/chicken/legumes and has a materially stronger modelled choline contribution. Do not credit A's eggs on egg-freeB2days or add eggs to B2 without chilling.
[NIH zinc](https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/Zinc-HealthProfessional/) and [selenium](https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/Selenium-HealthProfessional/) adult reference intakes require comparison with the **complete** diet, not this partial6.13mgZn/27.28µgSe mean. Bread/Pro+/fish missing fields can reverse apparent shortfalls. [NIH folate](https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/Folate-HealthProfessional/) distinguishes foodfolate from fortifiedfolicacid/DFE; the393.35µg partialmean is not proof of400µgDFEcoverage or of a deficit. No iodine/D value can be recovered from “high protein”.
[NIH vitaminD](https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/VitaminD-Consumer/) adult19–70 reference15µg/600IU and [iodine](https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/Iodine-Consumer/) adult150µg do not authorize supplement changes. Milk3147 is explicitly notDfortified and carries only2IU/100g; the400g sensitivity adds8IU, not600IU. Iodine is absent from this donor profile. Do not assume imported-food fortification, diagnose deficiency, increaseiodizedsalt to forcecoverage, or mark the alreadychosen supplements as taken.

**Design disposition:** individual B substitutions remain the intended use. The full-day/3day B2stress-test has knownenergy/protein differences, Pro+identity conflict and incomplete micro/sodium coverage; therefore it cannot be promoted to a standalone repeated diet. B1 requires safelychilled cookedAleftovers; it does not solve totalcookingfailure. These conclusions are robust across bothPro+macrobranches and the Tadımcalibration: none supplies previouslymissing micronutrient evidence. Omega-3/weekfish, sodium/SFA, actual coldchain, libraryfoodlocation, texture/volume and package/lifecycle conditions from earlier sections still apply unchanged. The appropriate correction is to retain the bounded fallback role and resume A when feasible, **not create additionalmandatoryfood/supplement slots**.

Validation:17liveprofiles fetched inonebatch; explicit units/missingness/duplicatecalcium checks; JS calculatedall21fields/3daymean, independentPythonDecimal reproduced high-consequencecholine/B12/Zn/Se results. Exact proof of remaining SKUfields requires actualspecification/label evidence; repeating thesame tables cannot close it. This closes the donor-transfer-analysis subtask; NUT02 finalcompleteadequacy stays OPEN with exactblockers, not a request for Ron to do calculations. Next independently executable work remains the outstanding price/lifecycle operands and final instruction audit after recipes arepromoted.

## 2026-11-03 — İçim UHT 200ml versus 1L: two-day cash, wastage and food-safety gate

**Bounded BUY-02/03 comparison; not a buy instruction, physical inventory claim or change to the existing B/A grams.** Public [ŞOK exact İçim %3 UHT 200 ml card, code 00172747](https://www.sokmarket.com.tr/icim-3-yagli-sut-200-ml-p-665652) displayed **23.90 TL** and 'Sepete Ekle'; public [ŞOK exact İçim %3 UHT 1 L card, code 11013025](https://www.sokmarket.com.tr/icim-sut-3-yagli-1-l-p-5218) displayed **64.90 TL** but **'Gelince Haber Ver' (not presently offered by that indexed online view)**. Both cards describe 3%-fat cow's milk, unopened ambient storage, and **refrigerate after opening, preferably consume within 2 days**. The index/search crawl was in late October/early November 2026; neither page establishes a Nov-03 checkout price, address inventory or delivery availability. An independent [Migros İçim 1-L product sheet](https://www.migros.com.tr/icim-sut-1-l-p-a80ba1) and [Migros 200-ml sheet](https://www.migros.com.tr/hemen/icim-sut-200-ml-p-a80ba3) also specify 3% milk and preferably a 2-day opened window. Migros's retailer nutrient table lists 57 kcal/2.9g protein per 100 ml, whereas the separately documented İçim manufacturer-declaration layer for 200ml uses 58kcal/3.1P: **do not silently change the B nutritional donor/label ledger on the basis of this pack-size comparison**.

| Exactly needed and safely consumed within ~2 days | Sealed 200-ml packs at indexed 23.90 TL each | One 1-L pack at indexed 64.90 TL | 1-L amount unused if nothing else consumes it |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| **400 ml** = 2 emergency B2 milk windows | **47.80 TL** | 64.90 TL | **600 ml** |
| **600 ml** = 3 × 200 ml | **71.70 TL** | 64.90 TL | **400 ml** |
| **800 ml** = 4 × 200 ml | **95.60 TL** | 64.90 TL | **200 ml** |
| **1000 ml** = 5 × 200 ml | **119.50 TL** | 64.90 TL | **0 ml** |

Arithmetic assumes every compared carton can actually be purchased at its indexed price **on the same channel**, and an entire 1-L pack may be purchased in one unit. For 400ml reserve-only, two small packs cost **17.10 TL less cash** and create no opened-carton remainder; they also keep separate portions factory-sealed until used. At 600ml or more **within the safe 2-day window** the 1-L sticker becomes arithmetically cheaper than 3+ separate 200-ml cartons, but has spoilage/waste, re-portioning, travel, storage and actual checkout burdens not captured by sticker price. At 800ml, index-price cash advantage is **30.70 TL** before these costs but 200ml remains unused if no other milk use. An unauthenticated indexed price and a 'notify when arrives' item are **not actionable stock**.

**B2 engineering decision:** retain the existing *two sealed 200-ml İçim cartons* as the conditional minimum portable/no-fridge reserve; this is **not an additional purchase**, and it avoids the unsafe assumption that opened 1L UHT remains shelf-stable. A 1-L home/fridge stock could be a conditional recurring alternative only if physical 0–4°C refrigeration, measured use/portion transport and substantial consumption inside the product's 2-day opened window are confirmed, AND actual checkout/delivery price is sufficiently lower. Do not pre-open 1L at home and carry the remainder for all-day ambient B2: that would turn the scenario into a cold-chain B1 dependency. If milk is already available inside diet A, moving two still-sealed small cartons to the reserve has **0 incremental purchase cash** but not zero economic food use; buying 1L solely for reserve is not justified at these indexed figures.

**Remaining UNKNOWN:** real Ron-scope current branch stock and checkout delivery, existing stock/opening time, actual home/work fridge temperatures, alternate 250-ml cartons, whether 600–1000 ml would reliably be consumed within 2 days, and exact label of the actually purchased pack. BUY-01/02/03, NUT-03, SYS-01 stay OPEN as applicable. No change to A/B grams, milk nutrition panel, caloric/day/3-day totals, reminders, diary, payment or purchase. The **2-day manufacturer/retailer opening guidance** governs against using an opened carton's assumed multi-day ambient life.

### Цена и lifecycle сравниваются с известным fırın-кандидатом
Историческая условная арифметика: ЕСЛИ та буханка действительно400г за40TL, то100TL/кг и10TL/100г; это не текущая цена и не измеренный выход. Формула для реальной буханки: cost100г=100×ценаTL/измереннаямассаГ. Сравнение с85.95/550=156.27TL/кгUNO — исторический условный контрпример, не сегодняшнее ранжирование.
Для B сначала использовать подходящие реально имеющиеся A-остатки/обычный удобный fırın-маршрут с сохранением роли белого/цельнозернового хлеба; отдельный пакетUNO не покупать автоматически ради совпадения модели. Точный публичныйSKU остаётся useful reference, но неизвестные данные местного хлеба не разрешают его забыть. Нарезка/масса ломтя/заморозка/сроки/соль пекарни проверяются только источником или фактическим измерением; новый визит/сообщение продавцу не выполнялись. Не обещать меньшийGI только от ekşi mayalı.
Реальные пробы/пачки/вес могут уточнить выбор, но отсутствие этих данных не блокирует остальную самостоятельную смету/микроработу. Никаких изменений A,диария,напоминаний,заказов или дополнительных хлебных обязательств нет.

Это scenario,неизмеренныйвыходRon. При покупке одногопоказанногоunitbread550+milk200+banana500 cash180.77TL,противменьшейconsumptionсуммы;покупнойминимальныйшагbanana иадреснаядоставкаUNKNOWN. Не создаватькорзинувместопроверки. Остаются470гхлеба ипокупнаячастьбанана;необязыватьдоедать/неоставлятьнарезанноебезхолода.
Это более цельный logistics-кандидат, чем сумма разныхпродавцов, НО всёещёнеадресно гарантированныйпредслот. По shelf milkCarrefourна1.10дешевле,ноэкстрапоездканеоправданаэтойразницейбезroute-timeevidence.

JSunit-cost и независимыйPythonDecimalсовпали. Procurement/pack-size→nutrients→safety/volume→cost→liveinstruction impactpass выполнен: нутриентныедельтынулевые по заявленнымполям,largerpackоткрываетwaste/TETTусловие,50г/80гпорциинеизменны,новыхнапоминаний/дневниковнет. NUT-01/02/BUY-01/03 остаютсяOPEN дляостальныхоперандов/микро/адреса; старыйобщийблокер«доступныйхлебне найден»теперьсужендоадресногоостатка/фактическойпачки.


## 2026-10-23 — B: единый ingredient-ledger и проверка зависимостей
**Статус: проверенный ограниченный расчёт, OPEN-ANALYSIS для финального меню.** Это работа ассистента под делегированием Ron, не Ron-originated выбор новой диеты. A, фактические дневники, дозы добавок и напоминания не изменены. Новый расчёт ниже разрешает округления/Na-конвенцию предыдущего handoff, но не превращает неизвестные этикеточные поля в нули.

JS direct ingredient-sum независимо воспроизведён Python Decimal из исходных карточек. Расхождения ~.003г с предыдущим delta-log — округлённая база126.9 вместо126.903 и подобное, не новая этикетка. В пользовательских цифрах достаточно целыхккал/десятыхг.

## 2026-10-22 — резерв B: проверенный расчётный handoff
**Датированный handoff: для сверенных B сумм/Na и150гYayla приоритет имеет раздел2026-10-23 выше. Старый220гдонор не exactSKU.**
Status: **OPEN-ANALYSIS / NOT A SECOND MANDATORY DIET**. Это предложения/модели ассистента, не факт покупки, наличия или еды и не новое решение Ron. Основной A, дневники, напоминания и дозы добавок не изменены. Предшествующие PASS для A не означают PASS полного B.

### Условный B1: только безопасные готовые остатки A
Улучшенный УСЛОВНЫЙ B1 «используем безопасные остатки A», без lor:

| Окно | Точный состав для модели | ккал | Б, г | клетчатка, г |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| 06:00 | 2 готовых яйца =100 г без скорлупы; UNO100 г; İçim200 мл; банан120 г без кожуры |637.6|26.1|4.3|
| 10:00 | миндаль30 г, грецкий орех15 г, UNO40 г, целое яблоко180 г съедобной части |472.1|11.9|9.5|
| 12:00 | готовый нут110 г без заливки, готовая куриная грудка150 г, UNO100 г отдельно, томат150 г, перец100 г, масло5 г, петрушка15 г |799.5|65.4|13.9|
| 16:45 | закрытый İçim200 мл, UNO80 г, банан120 г без кожуры |430|12.1|3.9|
| поздний ужин | готовая чечевица100 г + готовые макароны150 г + йогурт150 г + UNO50 г отдельно + морковь100 г + томат100 г + масло15 г + лён5 г |821.3|29.6|15.2|

Итого ~3160.5 ккал / Б145.1 / Ж97.2 / усвояемые У403.8 / клетчатка46.8 / насыщенные27.5 г. Учтённый натрий1758 мг без молока/добавленной соли и без SKU-поправки для нута; реальный общий натрий UNKNOWN. Это близко к owner-модели A (~3060–3080ккал/145Б/99Ж/45–46клетчатки), но НЕ доказывает одинаковую микронутриентную полноту. За 3 одинаковых дня модель даёт ~9482ккал/435г белка/~140г клетчатки; это только арифметическое повторение, НЕ недельный рыбный или микроаудит.

Исполнение/границы: всё уже приготовлено/охлаждено безопасно; если этого нет, это НЕ B1. Завтрак: яйца очистить, молоко выпить; перекус сухой+целый фрукт; обед холодный салат, курицу измельчить, томат/несколько капель лимона дают влагу, хлеб отдельно; ужин холодная миска/паста с йогуртом, морковь мелко натереть. Сборка каждого окна ориентировочно 1–5мин, фактическая скорость/вкус/объём — OPEN-EMPIRICAL, еда займёт дополнительное время. Обед ~530г влажных компонентов, fit в1.2л ещё не доказан. Курица/макароны/яйца/йогурт требуют непрерывной безопасной холодовой цепи; уже сохранённые правила <=4°C и времени без холода действуют. Испорченные/сомнительные остатки не спасать этим рецептом. Нужны вилка, герметичный контейнер, отдельный хлеб; без микроволновки выполнимо. Ужин ~600г влажной еды может оказаться тяжёлым перед сном; при двух неудачных пробах перераспределить, не заставлять доедать. Курица150г здесь ПЕРЕНОСИТСЯ из предназначенного A, не означает дополнительную закупку/готовку поверх A.

Donor food IDs для повторения (все на100г): яйца464674, миндаль451442, грецкий3391 (USDA; первоначальный branded результат отвергнут), яблоко5442, нут715 (boiled WITHOUT SALT, НЕ точный Duru/Yayla), чечевица456598, йогурт449843 (НЕ точная Birşah этикетка), томат455522, перец456934, морковь465045, петрушка456917, банан450856, лён453347, EVOO452649, курица463083, cooked pasta187. Американский total carbohydrate переведён в available вычитанием клетчатки; для UNO использовано label54.5 +fiber2.3 только во внутреннем total-carb слое, чтобы не вычесть её дважды.

B1 остаётся условным использованием остатков; микроаудит ниже сохраняет неизвестные D/йод/натрий, а недельный рыбный баланс ещё требует проверки.
- [ ] BUY-02, нут: проверен запасной кандидат Yayla700г https://www.migros.com.tr/yayla-haslanmis-nohut-700-g-p-8b0a3e ; состав57% готового нута+вода+соль, таблица129ккал/8Б/8клетчатки/0.5г соли на100г. 700г нетто НЕ означает700г слитого нута; явной массы без жидкости/основы таблицы не установлено. Производитель подтверждает ready-to-eat категорию https://yaylaagro.com/markalarimiiz/yayla-haslanmis . Не подменять им donor715 и не использовать подозрительно высокие микроцифры интернет-таблицы (Ca519/Fe18 на100г) для закрытия адекватности без уточнения. Точная этикетка съедаемой порции остаётся blocker для SKU-точного расчёта. Покупки/дневник/напоминания не изменялись.

День: ~3008.4ккал /126.9г белка /91.35г жира /403.48г усвояемых углеводов /47.85г клетчатки /22.35г насыщенных. Натрием учтено1570мг, БЕЗ молочных продуктов и реальной соли готового нута; полный натрий UNKNOWN.

Не назначать обязательный дорогой Pro+ и не переносить заявления о переносимости производителя на Ron.

Рыбный B2: вместо нута220г одна ЦЕЛАЯ Dardanel125г включая масло; в завтраке и обеде UNO100г заменить на Etimek Tuzsuz70г в каждом; в10:00 добавить банан120г. Официальный продукт кılçıksız: не кредитовать кальций generic sardine WITH BONES; A-дневник в этой работе не исправлялся.

### Минимальный резерв для отдельных пропущенных слотов
Проект:1герметичная Dardanel125г из недельнойA +1Etimek Tuzsuz148г +2закрытых İçim200мл. Не обязательный3суточный рацион; lor/Pro+/неоднозначный нут не обязательный склад. Целые фрукты/овощи — из реально имеющихся обычных запасов/JIT, наличие UNKNOWN. Рыбный слот: wholecan+сухари70г+томат150г; предтренировочный: milk200мл+сухари56г+банан120г. Остаток22г сухарей сухим закрыть; вторуюmilk-пачку не открывать заранее.
После использования восстановить исходный уровень при следующей ОБЫЧНОЙ закупке, сверив A-остатки; FIFO по срокам. Не дублировать автоматически набор дома и на работе. Вилка/миска для рыбы, хлеб отдельно. Перishable B1: <=4°C,2замороженных холодоэлемента→рабочий холодильник; >2ч суммарно без безопасного холода (>1ч при>32°C) не использовать. Закрытые B2 хранить по маркировке, повреждённые/вздутые банки не использовать.

NUT-01/02/04 OPEN-ANALYSIS: SKU/полныйNa/микро-недельный баланс и окончательная стоимость всех окон. BUY-01/03 OPEN: единый доступный маршрут, доставка, fruit/veg цены и edible yield. NUT-03/TEST OPEN-EMPIRICAL: реальные1–2 пробы, вкус/сухость, сборка/еда,1.2л fit,500мл вечерняя переносимость. >=2 неудачных проб одной замены либо B>=3слотов/неделю2недели — повод пересмотреть A, не наращивать обязательную диету.
SYS-03 CLOSED для шести известных Calendar-серий: устаревший not-started текст исправлен23.10 с live read-back. SYS-02 зависимая сверка закрыта в разделе B executor audit от23.10; новых ежедневныхB reminders/diary-prefill не создавать. Условие одобрения рецептов сохраняется. Локальная структура программы A и реальные logs не менялись.
Следующий самостоятельный шаг: разрешить точный доступныйPro+/нут/натрий, дозавершить стоимость окон; не генерировать новые B-варианты без новой существенной причины.

## Master-board operating rule

- **Work from this board, not from whichever topic was mentioned most recently.** Whole-system work advances the highest-value unresolved row that can currently be resolved.
- A new material factor discovered later is classified against `references/nutrition/ideal-nutrition-factor-map.md`. If genuinely new, extend the factor map, this board, and the dependency graph in the same architecture/continuity change.
- After any material food/amount/product/timing/cooking/schedule change, run the dependency graph before updating affected rows.
- Historical sections below preserve provenance and may contain superseded quantities; the board + newest dated decisions own current GitHub fallback.
- **Design closure is not execution closure.** The current design can be frozen while OPEN-EMPIRICAL rows are tested in reality; STALE-LIVE rows prevent claiming the whole operating system is fully rolled out.

### 2026-10-19 assistant working pilot — discretionary ready-food purchases (no execution confirmed)
Status: **PROPOSED / MEASUREMENT PENDING**, not a user-confirmed habit, completed behavior, or replacement of the adopted nutrition baseline.

- **Trigger/context:** Ron directly reported in his Oct-19 discussion that he sometimes buys döner/ready food even when safe prepared food is available at home; he agreed to testing practical usefulness of stronger research and decision methods generally. Choosing food spending as the first demonstration is the **assistant's working choice**, not Ron's explicit choice of intervention.
- **Working test window:** 2026-10-19 through 2026-10-25, only on actual opportunities where prepared home food is edible, safe, suitable and accessible. Prior to an unplanned ready-food purchase, use the existing prepared food as default; if unsuitable/unavailable or an intentional preference for buying, buying remains permitted and reason is logged without framing it as moral failure.
- **Minimum weekly evidence:** number of opportunities, number and actual TRY cost of unplanned purchases, reasons when home food was bypassed (lack of available food, taste/dryness, time/effort, hunger, social/preference or other), and any adverse nutrition/time/mood impacts. Do not infer savings from merely not buying; compare with reliable baseline purchases (if available) and include actual replacement-food cost or food waste. Avoid a new elaborate diary, duplicate TickTick task or Calendar schedule absent exact authorization. User may report one compact weekly summary.
- **External evidence:** implementation-intention meta-analysis of 642 tests (DOI 10.1080/10463283.2024.2334563) supports if-then planning in aggregate but is sensitive to motivation/context. 2025 umbrella review of 31 food-choice reviews (DOI 10.1186/s12916-025-04381-8) identifies promising environmental interventions but generally low confidence in underlying reviews; individual benefit remains **UNKNOWN** until execution.
- **Stop/revise rule:** if this generates extra distress, food restriction, meaningful hunger, spoilage or disproportionate effort, do not force it. If repeated bypass stems from disliked/dry meals or impractical cooking, redesign the food/default instead of blaming discipline. First report must distinguish data collected from actual reduction or improvement; 7 days are insufficient to claim durable habit formation.

### 2026-09-22 whole-lifecycle consistency audit — corrections and remaining empirical risks
Status: **DESIGN STRONG / LIVE TIMING RESIDUE + EXECUTION VALIDATION REMAIN**.

- Canonical execution interpretation when historical sections conflict: the Master Status Board plus the 2026-09-22 **weekly-prep timing correction** and **one-container execution simplification** override older Sep-21 operational details.

These are evidence/history, not current execution instructions.

A first-run batch allowed up to 120 min fits only if return travel + transition leaves enough of the 200-min 18:00–21:20 window; with dinner integrated, travel materially above ~80 min would force either a shorter first-run batch or split production. This is an execution check, not a reason to guess commute time.

Exact home cold-storage capacity and pan/stove throughput are not yet direct evidence. If capacity is tight, use fewer bulk containers and split production rather than unsafe deep-pot cooling.
- **No-scale execution:** pack fractions and repeatable household measures make the plan launchable without a kitchen scale, but nuts/flax/oil and cooked-yield portions are the highest-calorie/error-sensitive items. A cheap digital kitchen scale is a high-ROI optional simplifier, not a launch prerequisite.
- **Adherence/taste risk:** the nutrient architecture is intentionally repetitive (same oat breakfast, six pasta lunches, daily lentils). This lowers cost/cognitive load but makes monotony a genuine long-term adherence risk. First-line correction should be seasoning/texture/produce rotation without changing the nutrient architecture.
- **Late training dinner risk:** the current training-night dinner remains roughly a substantial meal rather than literally “light”; if fullness or sleep latency is repeatedly worse on two or more training nights, move ~150–250 kcal earlier in the day rather than reducing total daily intake.
- First-cycle measurement set is therefore expanded to: shopping duration, Saturday load, Sunday return-home time, Sunday batch active/elapsed time, cold-storage fit, morning punctuality, backpack burden, lunch volume/eating comfort, 16:45 dryness/palatability, training-night fullness/sleep, GI response and actual fallback frequency.
- These findings correct the prior overstatement that no meaningful pre-launch inconsistencies remained. Nutrient design is still strong; execution proof is not yet established.

### 2026-09-23 failure-recovery matrix
Status: **ASSISTANT WORKING PROTOCOL / PRESERVES SLEEP AND DATA INTEGRITY**.

- General rule: a failed meal/prep step is recorded as a failure/substitution, not hidden by forcing the missing calories into a huge late dinner. Preserve safety, punctuality, training/sleep and truthful logging before arithmetic perfection.
- **One-off breakfast failure:** leave home on time. Move the existing dry 10:00 food earlier and/or buy a simple breakfast near work; eat the boiled egg if transport is practical. Do not delay departure to finish the planned oat meal and do not automatically add the entire missed breakfast after 21:00.
- **Lunch spoiled/lost/no fridge:** discard unsafe food and use a simple bought meal centered on a substantial protein source + carbohydrate; log the actual item/estimate. Do not eat questionable chicken/yogurt merely to preserve the plan.
- **16:45 perishable portion unavailable:** the already-carried dry carbohydrate/fruit layer can preserve training fuel for one day; add a convenient safe protein source only if available without creating delay. One lower-protein feeding does not require redesigning the day.
- **Post-training dinner unavailable:** use a fast home fallback from safe existing staples (for example dairy + bread/whole-grain bread + fruit/nuts, or an available canned fish assembly) and log reality. Avoid cooking a full replacement meal late enough to damage the 22:00 sleep target.
- **Sunday prep substantially fails:** do not perform a midnight recovery cook. Use bought/simplified work meals for the affected early-week slots and restore the system at the next feasible prep block. Morning oat food can be made as a quick one-off if needed; the plan does not require pretending Sunday prep happened.
- **Wednesday top-up fails:** use safe bought/shelf-stable substitutions Thu–Sat as needed and resume at the next batch opportunity rather than sacrificing sleep on Wednesday night.
- **Missing SKU at purchase:** apply the existing nutrient/function substitution logic; exact brand is replaceable unless the substitute materially changes sodium, energy density, storage, meal volume or another controlled variable.
- **Repeated failure rule:** one isolated fallback is normal system resilience. The same failure mode occurring >=2 times in the first cycle is evidence that the architecture, not Ron's willpower, should be redesigned.

### 2026-09-23 explicit time-and-leverage audit
Status: **VISIBILITY DEFECT FIXED / EXECUTION-TIME ESTIMATES REMAIN EMPIRICAL**.

- Cooking time was always represented in the system (board #20 batch cooking and #26 time/ROI; factor-map #17 preparation/cooking engineering), but practical hacks/service leverage lived mostly in factor-map #16 and were not visible as a dedicated board row. That made closure look more complete than the execution surface actually was. Board #31 now makes it explicit.
- **Current working time budget, not measured fact:** Saturday eggs/setup ~5–10 min active / ~15–25 min elapsed; Sunday first block ~35–50 min active / ~60–90 min elapsed if chicken, pasta and lentils can overlap safely; Wednesday top-up ~25–40 min active / ~45–60 min elapsed; nightly next-day staging/packing ~5–10 min; morning porridge appliance time ~2–3 min plus ~8–10 min eating. The first real two runs own these numbers and may move them materially.
- High-value leverage/hacks already designed:
  - ask the butcher/seller to split the ~1.4-kg raw chicken into ~650 g + ~750 g and cut it into uniform pieces if the service exists;
  - use delivery/earlier purchase for heavy durable stock and reserve Saturday for perishables/last-mile items;
  - freeze bread in near-term portions instead of repeatedly managing mould/freshness;
  - cook the full weekly lentil amount once rather than repeating a second cook;
  - stage the next morning's dry oat portion and next-day dry snack components the previous evening;
  - keep fruit whole and bread/Etimek/nuts dry; only chilled perishables use cold-chain capacity;
  - use a basic digital scale or one repeatable household measure to eliminate repeated mental arithmetic for oil/nuts/flax/pasta;
  - use parallel stovetop tasks only when safe and when it actually reduces elapsed time rather than creating monitoring/cleanup overload;
  - cool in shallow/smaller portions so safety and refrigeration time are improved without extra waiting;
  - use seller preparation, pre-cut produce, cooked legumes or delivery only when saved recurring labor/route friction outweighs price/sodium/quality trade-offs.
- Further “lifehack” hunting is useful only if it removes repeated minutes, a purchase trip, cleanup, carrying weight, storage burden or a common failure mode. Novel tricks without measurable recurring leverage should not be added.

### 2026-09-23 Dardanel sardine label correction
Status: **MATERIAL DONOR-MAPPING DEFECT FOUND / MACRO EFFECT SMALL / LIVE CRONOMETER STALE**.

- Current exact product: Dardanel Zeytinyağlı Çanakkale Sardalya, **125 g net per can, 85 g drained fish per can**. Two weekly cans therefore represent **250 g total product but ~170 g drained fish**, not 250 g drained fish.
- Exact product label/official values per 100 g: **213 kcal, 21.76 g protein, 13.76 g fat, 0.4 g carbohydrate, 1.7 g salt**; Dardanel's official product page also reports **1420 mg EPA+DHA/100 g**.
- Current intended meal interpretation should be explicit: consume the **whole can including its allocated oil** unless Ron actually drains/discards oil. This matches the no-extra-oil sardine meals and exact product macros. If oil is discarded, the day must be logged differently.
- Hybrid evidence rule for future Cronometer correction: use the exact Dardanel full-product label for energy/macros/salt/omega-3; use a comparable sardine donor only for micronutrients the label does not declare, scaled to ~85 g fish/can rather than 125 g.
- Using the generic USDA-style drained-sardine macro donor as a bound, replacing 250 g/week generic drained sardines with 250 g/week exact Dardanel product changes the weekly average only about **+2 kcal/day, -1 g protein/day, +0.8 g fat/day and +0.1 g carbohydrate/day**. The ~3131-kcal launch controller therefore does not need a menu redesign.
- Fish-specific donor micronutrients are more affected by the 250->170 g fish-mass correction. Vitamin D is plausibly ~30 IU/day lower than the prior ~169-IU/day food model; calcium/B12/selenium donor contributions also fall proportionally, but the total diet remains broadly adequate. Exact product omega-3 is strong and directly labeled.
- **Authorization update 2026-09-23:** Ron explicitly instructed the assistant to fix everything currently fixable after both the yogurt-placement residue and sardine mapping defect had been surfaced. This authorizes these exact two Cronometer corrections. The current turn's tool surface does not expose the Cronometer connector, so the remaining blocker is live-tool availability plus mandatory write/read-back, not user permission. Do not ask Ron to repeat authorization for the same exact delta when the connector is available again.

### 2026-09-23 breakfast-prep simplification
Status: **ASSISTANT WORKING OPTIMIZATION / NUTRIENTS AND MORNING TIME BUDGET PRESERVED**.

- Multi-day cooked oat porridge is unnecessary overhead. Reheating a stored portion and microwave-cooking a pre-staged portion both consume roughly the same 2–3 min morning appliance window, while batch-cooking creates extra Sunday/Wednesday work, refrigerator volume, containers and texture degradation.
- Preferred execution: during the prior-evening pack, stage the next morning's ~80 g oat-flour portion in the microwave-safe breakfast bowl/container (use the same household measure if no scale). In the morning add ~250 ml milk, microwave ~2–3 min, then add the allocated flax/pekmez and eat with the boiled egg.
- This uses one reusable breakfast vessel rather than storing three wet breakfasts at once. If the morning cook proves slower/messier than expected on >=2 days, revert to pre-cooked portions; real execution owns the final choice.

### 2026-09-23 equipment and purchase-staging audit
Status: **PRE-LAUNCH FRICTION REDUCED / SMALL PHYSICAL GAPS REMAIN**.

- Confirmed launch assets already owned/reported: ~1.20-L work lunch container, two cold packs, home microwave, refrigerator/freezer and work refrigerator access. A thermobag and probe thermometer remain useful optional upgrades, not launch prerequisites.

Do not assume a commercial meal-prep container set is required: shallow household containers plus food-safe resealable freezer bags can satisfy the design. The only unresolved fact is whether enough such capacity already exists at home.

Without a scale the model remains launchable via package fractions/household measures, but execution error and mental bookkeeping are higher. This is not a claim Ron chose to buy one.
- Chicken precision can be partly outsourced at purchase: when practical, ask the butcher/seller to divide the weekly ~1.4-kg raw amount into approximately **650 g + 750 g** packs. This removes one of the hardest no-scale measurements; treat service availability as store-specific rather than guaranteed.
- One weekly lentil cook deliberately reduces no-scale complexity: only the weekly dry amount must be approximated/measured once instead of dividing it into Sunday/Wednesday raw sub-batches.
- Purchase staging should separate **durable stock** from **weekly perishables**. Durable/UHT/dry items (pasta, lentils, nuts, flax, Etimek, sardines, olive oil, iodized salt/spices, unopened UHT milk where inventory allows) can be acquired earlier or by delivery. Saturday/last-mile shopping should prioritize chicken, yogurt, bread and fresh produce. This avoids making one heavy post-work trip a hard dependency of the whole launch.
- Keep currently owned oat flour and pekmez as stock bridges; do not rebuy them by default. D3 remains outside the Day-1 purchase gate.

### 2026-09-23 lentil production simplification
Status: **ASSISTANT WORKING OPTIMIZATION / WEEKLY TOTAL UNCHANGED**.

- After fixing the Sunday double-provisioning arithmetic, a second comparison shows that two separate lentil cooks are still unnecessary.
- Preferred working architecture: cook the entire **~700 g cooked weekly lentil target once on Sunday**. Keep ~400 g for Sun–Wed refrigerated; freeze ~300 g for Thu–Sat after cooling; move the frozen late-week portion to the refrigerator Wednesday night.
- This preserves the same food/safety model, reduces Wednesday work and cleanup, avoids having to measure two awkward dry-lentil sub-batches without a kitchen scale, and costs only ~300 g of temporary freezer volume.
- Reopen only if real freezer capacity or thawed texture is poor.

### 2026-09-23 rolling-cycle lentil correction
Status: **ARITHMETIC DEFECT FIXED / WEEKLY NUTRIENT TOTAL UNCHANGED**.

- The previous split-batch wording accidentally scheduled ~400 g cooked lentils on Sunday (Sun–Wed) plus ~400 g on Wednesday (Thu–Sun). Once the cycle repeats, Sunday would be provisioned both by the prior Wednesday batch and the new Sunday batch, producing ~800 g cooked lentils for a 700-g/week plan.
- Correct recurring cycle: **Sunday ~400 g cooked lentils = Sun/Mon/Tue/Wed; Wednesday ~300 g = Thu/Fri/Sat.**
- This exactly reconciles to the Cronometer/meal target of **700 g cooked lentils/week**, removes a redundant freezer/thaw step, and slightly reduces Wednesday workload.
- The first launch week uses the same rule; no special Sunday carryover is required.

### 2026-09-23 launch runbook — Day 0 through first-cycle decision rules
Status: **ASSISTANT WORKING EXECUTION PROTOCOL / NOT YET RON-EXECUTED**.

**Day 0 readiness gates before 2026-09-28**
- Food needed for the first Mon–Wed block must already be physically available before the Sunday post-library prep. Do not depend on a late Sunday full-basket shop.
- Existing ~1.20-L work container clean/dry; two cold packs fully frozen; at least one food-safe resealable bag/wrap available for the 16:45 chilled chicken.
- Refrigerator/freezer must have enough practical space for the first block. Do not require a particular purchased container set, but cooling must be possible in shallow/small enough portions rather than leaving a deep hot pot to cool slowly.
- No D3 purchase is required for Day 1. Do not log or take a supplement merely because it exists in the model.
- Cronometer is nutrient/template support only until the stale Mon–Sat yogurt meal placement is successfully corrected and each real day is reconciled to actual intake.

**Saturday evening**
- Boil 8 eggs, cool/refrigerate in shell.
- Stage Sunday's dry oat portion in the breakfast bowl/container; no multi-day cooked-porridge batch is needed.
- Freeze both cold packs.
- Ensure Monday dry foods can be assembled without morning weighing/searching.
- If the main purchase is heavy or off-route, durable bulk stock should already have been obtained; Saturday can be limited to perishables/last-mile items.

**Sunday after library**
- Cook ~650 g raw chicken -> target ~440 g cooked for Mon/Tue/Wed; the full ~700 g cooked-lentil weekly batch (refrigerate ~400 g for Sun–Wed and freeze ~300 g for Thu–Sat after cooling); ~420 g dry pasta for Mon–Wed. Do not batch-cook weekday oat breakfasts.
- Use parallel cooking only where equipment safely permits it. Record start time, finish time and whether cleanup materially extended the block.
- Cool cooked food promptly in shallow/smaller portions; refrigerate Mon–Wed food. Build Monday lunch and dry-food pack before wind-down.
- If returning home late makes the full first block threaten the 22:00 sleep target, priority order is: Monday food -> Tuesday/Wednesday protein/lunch base -> remaining breakfast/top-up. Do not sacrifice sleep to preserve a paper-perfect batch.

**Mon–Sat morning**
- 06:00 wake; no measuring/searching from scratch.
- Add ~250 ml milk to the oat portion staged the prior evening, microwave ~2–3 min, then add the allocated flax/pekmez as applicable and eat within the established ~8–10 min target.
- Load already-chilled lunch + 16:45 chicken + two frozen cold packs + dry foods; depart 06:30.
- If breakfast causes departure after 06:30 twice in the first cycle, the home-breakfast timing design fails and must be changed; do not simply wake earlier by default.

**At work**
- On arrival, move all chicken/dairy/pasta-containing food directly to the refrigerator.
- ~10:00 dry snack; ~12:00 cold pasta lunch; ~16:45 afternoon/pre-training meal.
- Current one-container version has no yogurt at 16:45; that 50 g is consumed at home dinner.
- If the refrigerator is unavailable/warm or perishable food exceeds the conservative unrefrigerated time rule, discard/use the bought-meal fallback and log the real replacement.

**Training nights**
- 16:45 meal remains the main pre-training fuel; do not add a compensatory extra meal merely because dinner is late.
- After gym, use the pre-made dinner and preserve the 22:00 sleep target.
- If late-dinner fullness or sleep disruption occurs on >=2 training nights, move roughly 150–250 kcal from dinner to an earlier meal before considering any reduction in daily calories.

**Wednesday top-up**
- Cook ~750 g raw chicken -> target ~510 g cooked for Thu/Fri/Sat; ~420 g dry pasta for Thu–Sat.
- Move the frozen ~300 g Thu–Sat lentil portion from freezer to refrigerator; no second lentil cook or oat-breakfast batch is needed.
- Record total elapsed and active burden. If Wednesday repeatedly overruns the pre-sleep window, rebalance part of the work back to Sunday or simplify dish assembly rather than extending bedtime.

**Week-1 failure thresholds**
- Morning: departure delayed by breakfast/packing on >=2 days -> morning workflow redesign.
- Lunch: >30 min to eat or uncomfortable fullness/heaviness on >=2 days -> redistribute part of lunch energy earlier/later; do not force 140 g dry pasta.
- 16:45 meal: dry/unpleasant on >=2 exposures -> fix moisture/seasoning/assembly first.
- GI: repeated material bloating/urgency/pain or bowel disruption -> identify likely driver and reduce/ramp the relevant load rather than abandoning the whole architecture.
- Cold chain: any real temperature/holding failure -> use fallback; do not "count" the planned meal as eaten.
- Backpack: material shoulder/back discomfort or carrying friction on >=2 workdays -> reduce carried nonperishable bulk, use workplace stock where safe/appropriate, or change bag/transport architecture.
- Prep: either Sunday or Wednesday block repeatedly threatens sleep -> split/resequence production.
- Taste/monotony: same dish disliked twice -> vary seasoning/texture/produce before changing nutrient totals.

**End-of-day data integrity**
- A planned Cronometer day becomes execution evidence only after actual intake is reconciled.
- Exact adherence can be confirmed explicitly; substitutions/missed amounts must replace the planned record rather than remain hidden.
- No inference from a prefilled row to "Ron ate it".

**~2-week calibration**
- Treat nutrition + return to ordinary 4-day training as one integrated baseline.
- Review bodyweight trend, waist, training performance, appetite, sleep and adherence.
- Change energy in ~150-kcal steps only if the repeated outcome trend supports it; after baseline, change one material variable at a time.

### 2026-09-22 fourth lifecycle audit — split-batch correction
Status: **ASSISTANT WORKING CORRECTION / WEEKLY FOOD TOTALS UNCHANGED / NOT YET EXECUTED**.

- Whole-lifecycle comparison reverses the temporary all-week-Sunday chicken/lentil choice. With Sunday library 09:00–18:00, unknown return travel, a 22:00 sleep target, unverified freezer/container capacity and a free Wednesday evening, concentrating all weekly protein production on Sunday creates more schedule and storage fragility than it removes.
- Revised intended production: Sunday cooks ~650 g raw chicken -> ~440 g cooked for Mon/Tue/Wed; the full ~700 g cooked-lentil weekly batch; ~420 g dry pasta for Mon–Wed; and Mon–Wed oat breakfasts. Keep ~400 g lentils Sun–Wed refrigerated and freeze ~300 g for Thu–Sat. Wednesday cooks ~750 g raw chicken -> ~510 g cooked for Thu/Fri/Sat; ~420 g dry pasta for Thu–Sat; and Thu–Sat oat breakfasts, then moves the frozen lentils to the refrigerator.

- This change does not alter Cronometer nutrient totals, shopping quantities or meal composition. It reduces Sunday elapsed work, freezer volume, thaw bookkeeping and the number of days cooked chicken spends frozen, at the cost of a materially larger Wednesday top-up. The first measured Sunday and Wednesday runs decide whether this split remains superior.

### 2026-09-22 third lifecycle audit — procurement mass and launch confounding
Status: **EXECUTION MODEL REFINED / FOOD TOTALS UNCHANGED**.

- Modeled recurring food mass is about **~14 kg/week consumed**, with roughly **~12 kg/week** concentrated in dairy, chicken, fruit/vegetables, eggs and bread before packaging. A deliberately stocked first checkout can approach roughly **~19 kg** depending pack sizes because it also prepays eggs/dry stock/oil. These are planning masses, not a claim about the eventual bags.
- Therefore "one consolidated Saturday shop" is not automatically the highest-ROI physical route. Purchase-time routing should compare delivery vs a nearby store vs separating durable bulk stock from weekly perishables; avoid carrying a large basket through a long public-transport commute merely to save a trivial price difference.
- Ron's prior direct plan links food launch with resuming the ordinary 4-day strength program. This is coherent with the ~3131-kcal target activity state, but it means week-1 symptoms/performance cannot be causally assigned to diet alone. Treat the first ~2 weeks as one integrated launch baseline; subsequent corrections follow the one-material-variable rule.

### 2026-09-22 second lifecycle audit — first-launch boundaries and Sunday library
Status: **ADDITIONAL EXECUTION DEFECTS IDENTIFIED / BASE FOOD TOTALS UNCHANGED**.

- **Rami Library food rule:** current official Rami rules state that food is not consumed inside library-use areas; water is the general exception, and closed-cup/thermos drinks are allowed in study halls. Therefore the Sunday 12:00 sardine/Etimek/tomato meal and 16:45 snack must be treated as deliberate breaks outside the reading/use area or in another area where consumption is permitted. Do not model them as desk eating. Exact permission for bringing outside food into the cafe seating area was not established, so do not assume it.
- Sunday library foods are otherwise transport-robust because the sardine can remains sealed until eating and the planned Etimek/nuts/whole fruit/tomato do not require the work-fridge architecture. Sardine odor/mess is an adherence/social-friction check; open only immediately before the meal and dispose/close waste promptly.
- **Recurring-cycle boundary:** the physical prep cycle is Saturday/Sunday -> following Saturday, while Cronometer reporting is Monday -> Sunday. This is not a nutrient error but it matters for inventory. Example: the 8 eggs boiled on Saturday cover Sunday (2) + Mon–Sat (1/day); the following Sunday's 2 eggs come from the next Saturday batch. The same rolling-cycle interpretation applies to Sunday lentil dinner. Do not expect one physical Saturday batch to map 1:1 onto one Mon–Sun Cronometer week.
- **Procurement gate for launch:** the food design assumes launch groceries needed for Monday are physically home by Saturday night or otherwise before the Sunday post-library batch. Exact store route/duration is not proven. If a main shop is not conveniently on-route Saturday, buy durable stock earlier and reserve Saturday for perishables/last-mile items, or use an equivalent low-friction route; do not make Sunday evening both the full shopping trip and the full first batch.
- **Assistant-selected late-Sunday fallback:** if Sunday return home leaves insufficient time for the normal all-week chicken/lentil batch without sacrificing the 22:00 sleep target, cook only the Mon–Wed minimum (about 420 g dry pasta, ~650 g raw chicken for ~440 g cooked, ~400 g cooked lentils including Sunday dinner, and Mon–Wed oat breakfasts) and restore the larger Wednesday top-up for Thu–Sun. This is a contingency, not a new default.
- **Carried-load estimate:** a normal training workday carries roughly ~1.2–1.3 kg of food before container/cold-pack weight (large pasta lunch + dry 10:00 snack + 16:45 chicken/bread/tomato/banana). This is plausible but not negligible across a long commute; backpack comfort becomes a week-1 empirical criterion rather than an assumed nonissue.
- Weekly recipe arithmetic rechecked after the one-container yogurt move: milk 2.8 L, yogurt 1.05 kg, eggs 8, pasta 840 g dry, chicken 950 g cooked, lentils 700 g cooked, Nimet 700 g, white bread 620 g, Etimek 222 g, sardines 250 g, olive oil 200 g, almonds 240 g, walnuts 105 g, flax 35 g, grapes 1.26 kg, banana 840 g edible, pear/apple 350 g, carrots/tomatoes/green pepper 700 g each, parsley 105 g, oat flour 560 g and molasses 70 g. No arithmetic mismatch was found in those weekly controls.
- Day-level energy distribution is not perfectly proportional to training load (Wednesday is one of the higher-energy days), but the differences are small enough that weekly intake and actual body/performance response dominate; no carb/calorie cycling redesign is justified before execution evidence.

## Sep-19 full micronutrient audit COMPLETE — corrective changes
- A full seven-day average micronutrient model is now complete; details are in `history/2026-09-18-nutrition-launch-a-z.md`, section 17.
- Conservative modeled daily average before corrections: vitamin A ~1320 µg RAE; C ~134 mg; D ~3.9 µg; E ~18.3 mg; K ~93 µg; B1 ~2.1 mg; B2 ~3.1 mg; niacin ~33 mg; B5 ~9.1 mg; B6 ~2.8 mg; biotin ~69 µg (lower confidence); folate ~496 µg; B12 ~8.0 µg; choline ~603 mg; calcium ~1777 mg; iron ~20 mg; magnesium ~640 mg; phosphorus ~2700 mg; potassium ~4790 mg; sodium ~1650 mg; zinc ~15.7 mg; copper ~2.7 mg; manganese ~8.9 mg; selenium ~228 µg; ALA ~2.35 g; linoleic acid ~21.2 g; EPA+DHA ~0.35 g/day; fiber ~46 g/day.
- **Clear food-only gap:** vitamin D. Current food model reaches only ~25-30% of the modern 15 µg / 600 IU adult reference when Turkish dairy is not assumed fortified. Use a low-dose D3 solution aimed at the missing ~400-500 IU/day average; do not default to high-dose supplementation.
- **Small gap:** vitamin K. Add **~70 g fresh parsley/week (~10 g/day average)** or equivalent leafy greens. This is now part of the default fresh-produce layer and should be included in procurement.
- **Iodine:** likely adequate from daily milk/yogurt/eggs plus sardines, but exact Turkish-product iodine is too variable for a guaranteed number. Use iodized table salt if salt is used; do not add iodine/kelp supplement by default and do not add extra salt solely for iodine.
- **No supplement need identified:** K2, iron, selenium, zinc, magnesium, B-complex/multivitamin. Avoid selenium/manganese supplementation specifically because food intake is already high (~228 µg selenium; ~8.9 mg manganese) though still below adult ULs.
- Chromium/fluoride remain composition/environment-sensitive rather than actionable diet gaps; molybdenum/chloride are strongly/likely covered.
- **Post-correction status:** no remaining known essential-nutrient deficiency in the modeled diet that justifies a menu redesign. Remaining uncertainty is measurement/product variability, not a known gap.

## Sep-19 micronutrient audit methodology locked
- Final micronutrient audit will use a **hybrid evidence model**:
  1. exact brand/manufacturer labels for calories/macros/sodium/sugars and any micronutrients actually declared;
  2. NCCDB/USDA/TürKomp or comparable laboratory composition data for natural-food micronutrients and for missing fields when the donor is genuinely comparable;
  3. fields that cannot be transferred reliably remain **UNKNOWN**, never zero.
- Cronometer is the preferred calculation surface when its connector/live data are available, but branded Cronometer entries are not automatically complete: a branded record can be precise for label nutrients while missing iodine, choline, vitamin K, manganese or other unlabelled nutrients.
- Audit scope is full, not only suspected gaps: vitamins A, C, D, E, K; B1, B2, B3, B5, B6, B7, B9, B12; choline; calcium, iron, magnesium, phosphorus, potassium, sodium, zinc, copper, manganese, selenium, iodine; essential fatty acids/omega-3 and omega-6; fiber; protein and essential-amino-acid adequacy.
- Compare the **7-day average intake** and relevant episodic nutrients against adult-male reference intakes. Also check excess/UL risk where applicable.
- The current menu must not be called 'micronutrient-closed' until this full pass is complete.

## Sep-19 COMPLETE micronutrient closure audit — baseline revised
- Full quantitative 7-day audit is complete. Dated artifact: `history/2026-09-19-nutrition-complete-micronutrient-audit.md`.
- This corrects an earlier process defect: micronutrient closure should have been completed before describing the diet as nearly finished.
- **Material findings:** vitamin D food intake was ~9.5 µg/day vs 15 µg reference; vitamin K was ~98 µg/day vs 120 µg male AI; ALA was not robustly above 1.6 g/day; iodine was more plausibly **high/uncertain** than low because Turkish milk/yogurt iodine is substantial; choline and selenium are adequately covered.
- **Long-term baseline changes now supersede the Sep-08 quantities:**
  - UHT yarım yağlı milk **400 mL/day** (was 500);
  - tam yağlı yogurt **150 g/day** (was 300);
  - dry pasta **~640 g/week** (was 500);
  - cooked chicken **~950 g/week** (was 850);
  - fresh parsley **15 g/day / ~105 g/week** added;
  - ground flaxseed **5 g/day / ~35 g/week** added;
  - vitamin D3 **400 IU / 10 µg daily** added as a nutrient-closure dose; no automatic K2, iodine or multivitamin.
- Revised model is approximately **3099 kcal / 153 g protein / 100 g fat / 404 g carbohydrate / 47 g fiber**, essentially on the 3100/148.55/99.03/403.625 launch target.
- Revised modeled key micros: Ca ~1527 mg, Fe ~19.2 mg, Mg ~665 mg, K ~4915 mg, Zn ~15.1 mg, Se ~101 µg Türkiye-adjusted, folate ~522 µg, B12 ~4.1 µg, choline ~658 mg, A ~1161 µg RAE-equivalent, C ~166 mg, D ~17 µg total, E ~23.3 mg, K ~153 µg.
- **Iodine rule:** do not supplement iodine or deliberately add iodized salt to chase iodine. A 2023 Turkish ICP-MS study found high/variable iodine in commercial milk/yogurt; revised dairy quantity creates more margin. Exact Moova/Mis/Dardanel iodine remains batch/brand-UNKNOWN.
- **Procurement quantities are now stale where they depend on old menu:** milk becomes ~2.8 L/week, yogurt ~1.05 kg/week, pasta ~640 g/week, cooked chicken ~950 g/week plus conversion to raw yield, and add parsley ~105 g/week + flax ~35 g/week + low-dose D3. Re-optimize purchase sizes/prices before the next checkout total is called final.
- Status: **MICRONUTRIENT-CLOSED WORKING BASELINE / NOT YET EXECUTION-VERIFIED**. No conventional nutrient deficit is currently known; empirical tolerance/outcome and exact iodine remain the main uncertainty.

## Sep-19 long-term adequacy audit — base is strong, not yet proven 100% complete
- **Decision:** the current launch diet is suitable as a long-term **base pattern**, but it must not be represented as a literally immutable lifelong diet that has already proven every human nutritional need. Energy requirements, training load, body-mass trajectory, heat/sweat, health state, product formulations and seasonality can change.
- **Strongly covered / low concern by current pattern:** energy/macros, protein quality/distribution, fiber, calcium, iron, magnesium, selenium, B12 and broad B-vitamin coverage are strongly supported by the daily dairy + eggs + chicken/fish + lentils + oats/nuts + fruit/vegetable structure. Sardines twice weekly plus walnuts provide a strong omega-3 pattern. Exact labels still win over generic databases.
- **Vitamin D: unresolved, not automatically deficient.** Adult reference target is 15 µg / 600 IU/day. Turkish TürKomp shows meaningful vitamin D in whole egg, full-fat yogurt and canned sardine, but exact Moova yarım yağlı UHT / chosen yogurt / Dardanel product composition and sun exposure are not yet verified enough to guarantee the target. Do not auto-prescribe D3 solely from the planning model.
- **Iodine: unresolved.** Adult reference target is 150 µg/day. Dairy, egg and fish may contribute, but exact iodine values are not reliably present on product labels and the diet intentionally minimizes salt. Turkish iodized table salt is a useful tool but should not be added blindly until the total sodium/salt and product iodine picture are reconciled.
- **Vitamin K: likely improvable / not yet proven.** Adult male AI is 120 µg/day; current vegetables contribute some, but the baseline lacks a guaranteed daily leafy-green source. Fix by using the already-planned vegetable-rotation layer: replace part of the ~300 g/day vegetables with leafy greens / parsley / rocket / spinach / cabbage several times weekly rather than adding a second menu.
- **Choline: likely near target but not yet proven.** Adult male AI is 550 mg/day. Eggs, chicken, fish and dairy are all strong contributors, so supplementation is not justified without a full nutrient total.
- **Potassium:** current pattern is plausibly near the 3,400 mg/day adult male AI because of dairy, fruit, vegetables, lentils and potatoes are not required; exact total should be verified from the final product model rather than assumed.
- **Long-term operating rule:** keep the core architecture stable but rotate produce seasonally, maintain the fish/legume/nut/dairy/egg structure, and recalibrate calories based on measured bodyweight/waist/training outcomes. A fixed 3,100-kcal number is not a lifelong constant.
- **What remains before calling the diet 'micronutrient-closed':** one full exact-product micronutrient pass using final labels + a high-quality composition database, with special attention to vitamin D, iodine, vitamin K, choline and potassium. Only then decide whether any food substitution or supplement is warranted.

## Sep-19 pack-size optimization completed
- Full current basket has now been re-evaluated by unit price, consumption horizon, shelf/open life, freezer/pantry burden, trip frequency and cash tied up. This closes the pack-size pass that was previously left incomplete.
- **Eggs:** Ucuzradar currently shows M-size 30-packs at **169 TL (~5.63 TL/egg)** across BİM/ŞOK, versus the Work-captured M 10-pack at 70 TL (7 TL/egg). Default **30 M eggs** when date/condition are good; ~3.75 weeks at ~8 eggs/week.
- **Milk:** 4 x 1 L Moova UHT yarım yağlı at 39.50 TL/L remains the practical buy. Buying 8+ individual liters does not lower unit price, so extra inventory is not automatically useful.
- **Yogurt:** 3 kg at 192 TL (~64 TL/kg) narrowly beats 2 x 1.5 kg at 195 TL; use one 3 kg tub when label/date and refrigerator fit are good.
- **Chicken:** 4 kg ESK sırtsız göğüs at 109.90 TL/kg is the default stock-up (~3.2-3.6 weeks) because freezer storage is available and the price is strong; more is unnecessary without a further unit-price discount.
- **Red lentils:** 2 kg at 46 TL/kg (~6-7 weeks) is enough; no current larger-pack unit-price advantage is established.
- **Pasta:** 4 kg at local MarketFiyatı 15 TL/500 g (=30 TL/kg) when available; ~8 weeks. Current Ucuzradar fallback is ~17 TL/500 g, so the local MF offer is materially better.
- **Almonds:** current 150 g packs around 109 TL imply ~726.7 TL/kg and no confirmed larger-pack discount in Ucuzradar/MF. Buy **600 g (4 packs)** for ~2.5 weeks; buying more packs does not improve unit cost.
- **Walnuts:** BİM Simbat kırık ceviz içi 400 g / 219 TL (~547.5 TL/kg) dominates 150 g / 99 TL (~660 TL/kg). Buy one 400 g pack (~3.8 weeks).
- **Nimet Tuzsuz:** 6 x 330 g = 1.98 kg (~2.8 weeks) at 38.50 TL each when found; freeze extras. More packs give no unit discount and compete with chicken for freezer space.
- **White bread:** ~1.0-1.1 kg weekly; do not stock heavily because freshness/freezer burden dominate and no bulk discount is established. Toast bread remains only a ceiling/fallback, not the required form.
- **Eti Etimek Tuzsuz:** 6 x 148 g = 888 g, about 4 weeks. Current local MF fallback 65 TL each; cheaper Ucuzradar shelf-check can replace it.
- **Exact zeytinyağlı sardines:** 4 x 125 g = ~2 weeks at 117.71 TL/can. No confirmed multi-can unit discount in Ucuzradar/MF, so do not tie up cash in 8 cans by default.
- **Natürel sızma olive oil:** 1 L at 209.50 TL Ucuzradar target; about 4-5 weeks at current use. No confirmed larger natürel-sızma pack with better unit economics in the current source pass.
- **Fresh produce:** weekly only; season/quality/spoilage dominate bulk economics.
- **Base first checkout under these optimized quantities:** approximately **3,465 TL** before optional spices, using 30 eggs, 4 kg chicken, 4 kg pasta, 600 g almonds, 400 g walnuts, 6 Nimet, 6 Etimek, 4 exact zeytinyağlı sardines, 1 L olive oil, and one week's produce. This is inventory checkout, not weekly consumption cost.
- **Current weekly consumption-equivalent estimate under these prices:** about **1,486-1,502 TL/week**, mainly varying with 1.1-1.25 kg raw chicken. This remains a planning estimate until checkout labels/quantities are observed.


## Sep-19 procurement selector correction — pack-size first principles
- Never interpret "weekly consumption" as "buy one week's quantity." For every shelf-stable/freezable/long-life item, compare all reasonable pack sizes on **effective unit cost** and choose the purchase horizon that minimizes total recurring cost without creating meaningful spoilage, storage, freshness, cash-flow or adherence penalties.
- Default comparison fields: TL/kg or TL/L or TL/item; weeks of supply at Ron's planned consumption; shelf/open-life; freezer/pantry burden; discount magnitude; cash tied up; extra-trip frequency.
- Larger packs are often but **not automatically** better: buy larger when the unit-price advantage survives the storage/spoilage/cash test. Examples from current evidence: eggs 30-pack likely beats 10-pack if date is good; 400 g walnut kernels beats 150 g on TL/kg; 3 kg yogurt only slightly beats 2x1.5 kg and open-life/freshness can matter; almonds showed no bulk discount in the current Work capture, so more inventory is not justified merely for size.
- Apply this rule across the **entire basket** before the next checkout total is called final.

# Ron Nutrition — current-state canon

Updated: 2026-09-18 Europe/Istanbul
Status: **REVISED PROPOSAL SAVED / LOCAL OFFERS PARTLY VERIFIED / NOT ACTIVATED / EXECUTION UNVERIFIED**


## Pre-launch optimization CLOSED — 2026-09-18
- A final cheap/high-ROI pre-launch pass found **no remaining menu/architecture redesign worth delaying Day 1**.
- **Morning default locked:** hot breakfast is eaten at home. Carrying reheated food warm to work is an optional exception, not the baseline; it does not remove the cold-chain need for later perishable meals and adds timing/temperature complexity.
- **Cold transport verification rule added:** pre-chilled work portions -> insulated insert/lunch bag -> two frozen cold sources -> immediate work refrigerator. Once a thermometer is available, one representative commute test should measure the food on arrival before refrigeration; about **<=4.4°C / 40°F** is the simple pass signal for that route/packing/weather. Retest only after a materially hotter/longer transport condition.
- **First two prep runs are calibration runs:** record elapsed and rough active minutes. Repeated burden > the current 90–120 min elapsed expectation should trigger workflow/service simplification rather than sacrificing sleep.
- **Taste/ergonomics pre-commitment:** week 1 cold pasta default = yogurt + lemon + paprika + measured oil + water, chicken cut small across grain; curry-yogurt is the single fallback flavor. Redesign only if real execution shows dryness, mess, poor cold texture, excessive eating time or force-feeding.
- **GI rule:** do not pre-emptively cut the modeled ~42 g/day fiber without evidence; use the existing lentil-reduction fallback first if discomfort occurs, then reassess only if persistent.
- **Equipment decision revised after ROI review (2026-09-19):** do **not** require buying a dedicated insulated lunch bag or probe thermometer before launch. Ron already owns two cold packs and the intended route is chilled food -> roughly commute-length transport -> immediate work refrigerator. Dedicated insulation remains a useful safety margin, not a mandatory purchase. Keep unrefrigerated perishable time well below the ordinary 2-hour limit and, when ambient temperature is above ~32°C, well below the 1-hour limit; on hot/delayed days use a fresh-bought/ambient-safe fallback rather than forcing the packed meal. A probe thermometer remains the gold-standard verification tool for chicken doneness/cold-chain measurement, but current expected value is too low to make it a required purchase; cook poultry conservatively and use a safely cooked fallback whenever doneness is genuinely uncertain.
- Full closure details are in `history/2026-09-18-nutrition-launch-a-z.md`, section 16. Remaining uncertainty is now empirical rather than a reason for further pre-launch design.


## Sep-19 source-role clarification — Ucuzradar vs MarketFiyatı
- **Ucuzradar:** use as the broad price/store discovery source — which chain/store sells the item and at what current listed price.
- **MarketFiyatı:** use for the same price/store comparison **plus address-level local stock/availability** around Ron's chosen location.
- Operational rule: first identify attractive price/store candidates from Ucuzradar; then prefer/validate MarketFiyatı when exact nearby stock matters. A Ucuzradar offer remains a valid shelf-check candidate even when MarketFiyatı has no matching local-stock result; it is simply not stock-confirmed in advance.

## Sep-19 final procurement decision after Work result
- Work-mode output is now treated as price/stock evidence, not as the selector. Selection is corrected manually for total-system ROI.
- **Core route:** nearby A101 + ŞOK + BİM + Tarım Kredi; add Migros only for the exact zeytinyağlı sardine / convenient Etimek fallback. Avoid a separate Carrefour/Hakmar stop for only a few TL.
- **Buy-now quantities / choices:**
  - eggs: **pack-size optimization is mandatory.** The 10-pack at 70 TL is 7 TL/egg. A 30-pack M candidate at 169 TL is ~5.63 TL/egg and lasts ~3.75 weeks at ~8 eggs/week, so if Ucuzradar/MarketFiyatı confirms it and the date/condition are good, the 30-pack is the rational default. The earlier choice of 10 was an assistant optimization error, not a Work error.
  - milk: A101 Moova UHT yarım yağlı 1 L x4 at 39.50 TL if on shelf; Tarım Kredi 39.90 TL local-MF fallback.
  - yogurt: ŞOK Mis tam yağlı 3 kg at 192 TL. Carrefour 2x1.5 kg =195 TL is fresher-by-pack but not worth a special stop; use it only if already there.
  - chicken: Tarım Kredi / ESK Piliç Sırtsız Göğüs local MF 109.90 TL/kg; buy 4 kg if date/condition/freezer capacity are good, minimum 2 kg otherwise.
  - red lentils: TMO 1 kg x2 at 46 TL/kg via local MF/Tarım Kredi.
  - pasta: use local MF 15 TL/500 g if its confirmed shop is already on route; otherwise BİM Arbella 17 TL/500 g. Buy 4 kg only while this price band remains; 16 TL total premium for BİM is acceptable to avoid an extra stop.
  - almonds: 150 g raw packs at 109 TL; no bulk-unit discount in current Work data. Buy 600 g (4 packs) rather than 1 kg+ inventory; this covers ~2.5 weeks and preserves cash.
  - walnuts: BİM Simbat kırık ceviz içi 400 g at 219 TL (~547.5 TL/kg), dominates 150 g/99 TL (~660 TL/kg) for recurring use.
  - Nimet Tuzsuz Ekşi Mayalı Tam Buğday 330 g: A101 38.50 TL Ucuzradar target; local MF not confirmed. Check shelf; if found, buy 6 and freeze extras.
  - ordinary white bread: exact slot remains functional, not toast-specific. Prefer ordinary soft white bread if suitable/cheap; Uno Anadolu Tost 550 g x2 at 49.50 TL is only a packaged ceiling/fallback, not a requirement.
  - Eti Etimek Tuzsuz 148 g: buy where convenient; Carrefour Ucuzradar 56.90 TL is cheapest found, MF local result 65 TL is acceptable if it avoids an extra Carrefour stop. Up to 6 packs for ~4 weeks.
  - sardines: the 95 TL A101 product is plain Dardanel Sardalya and must not be assumed zeytinyağlı; Dardanel has separate olive-oil and sunflower-oil SKUs. Use exact Dardanel Zeytinyağlı Çanakkale Sardalya 125 g, Work/Migros 117.71 TL, 4 cans for ~2 weeks unless the A101 physical label confirms the zeytinyağlı SKU.
  - olive oil: A101 Kırlangıç natürel sızma 1 L 209.50 TL Ucuzradar target; if absent, local MF Güven Asa natürel sızma 1 L 249 TL.
  - produce weekly: seedless grapes 1.26 kg (BİM 35/kg target), banana ~0.48 kg (BİM yerli 79/kg), pear or apple ~0.6 kg (BİM 59/kg), carrot 0.7 kg (A101 29.50/kg target), tomatoes 0.7 kg (local MF Rio Tarla 19.90/kg), green pepper 0.7 kg (BİM 99/kg), lemon ~2–3 pieces (~0.3 kg; Tarım Kredi 57.90/kg target).
  - spices: only if missing at home; exclude from base checkout until inventory is known.
- **Ucuzradar rule:** Ucuzradar-only cheaper offers remain actionable shelf-check targets. Lack of MF confirmation means "check on shelf", not "reject". MF-confirmed local offer is the fallback when the Ucuzradar target is absent.
- **Approximate base checkout under the current stock-up quantities:** ~3.38k TL using conservative white-bread ceiling, MF 65 TL Etimek x6, exact Migros zeytinyağlı sardines x4 and 4 kg chicken. Actual can be lower if ordinary white bread / Carrefour Etimek / 15-TL pasta source is used; spices excluded.
